==================== [C:\Users\admin\Desktop\練習\MyPOS\pos-v1.1\server.js] ====================
// ---------------------------------------- [模組引入與中介軟體設定] 開始 ----------------------------------------
const express = require('express');
const http = require('http');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const WebSocket = require('ws');

const app = express();
const server = http.createServer(app);
const wss = new WebSocket.Server({ server: server });

app.use(express.text({ type: ['text/*', 'application/json', '*/*'] }));
app.use((req, res, next) => {
  if (typeof req.body === 'string') {
    try {
      req.body = JSON.parse(req.body);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  next();
});

app.use(express.json());
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));

const publicPath = path.join(__dirname, 'public');
if (fs.existsSync(publicPath)) {
  app.use(express.static(publicPath));
}
// ---------------------------------------- [模組引入與中介軟體設定] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [常數定義與初始資料庫配置] 開始 ----------------------------------------
const GAS_URL = "https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbxTGrRGwx5aNc6LDKOkevrQVRN4Q_n8ERZqzUgi59cuALgED4pIjkcNMFDOIdQIrkcm/exec";
const DB_FILE = path.join(__dirname, 'pos_database.json');

const initialDatabase = {
  menu: [],
  orders: [],
  settings: {
    openTime: "06:00",
    closeTime: "13:00",
    closedDaysOfWeek: "3",
    autoAccept: "關閉",
    enableDelivery: "開啟"
  },
  systemPassword: "1234",
  announcement: "",
  employees: ["店長", "早班夥伴"],
  clockStatus: {},
  expenseQuickWords: ["買菜", "買蛋", "瓦斯費", "水電費", "雜支", "叫油"],
  incomeQuickWords: ["外送補貼", "店販零售", "其他收入"],
  messages: []
};
// ---------------------------------------- [常數定義與初始資料庫配置] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [本機資料庫讀寫與推播工具] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function loadDB() {
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(DB_FILE)) {
      fs.writeFileSync(DB_FILE, JSON.stringify(initialDatabase, null, 2));
      return initialDatabase;
    }
    const content = fs.readFileSync(DB_FILE, 'utf-8');
    return JSON.parse(content);
  } catch (e) {
    return initialDatabase;
  }
}

function saveDB(data) {
  try {
    fs.writeFileSync(DB_FILE, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), 'utf-8');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('寫入資料庫失敗：', e);
  }
}

function broadcast(msg) {
  const payload = JSON.stringify(msg);
  wss.clients.forEach(client => {
    if (client.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      client.send(payload);
    }
  });
}
// ---------------------------------------- [本機資料庫讀寫與推播工具] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [時間格式化與單號輔助工具] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function normalizeTime(t) {
  if (!t) return "06:00";
  let parts = t.toString().trim().split(':');
  if (parts.length === 2) {
    let h = parts[0].padStart(2, '0');
    let m = parts[1].padStart(2, '0');
    return `${h}:${m}`;
  }
  return t;
}

function cleanNum(num) {
  if (!num) return '';
  return String(num).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').padStart(3, '0');
}
// ---------------------------------------- [時間格式化與單號輔助工具] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [Google試算表同步核心邏輯] 開始 ----------------------------------------
async function sendToGoogle(payload) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(GAS_URL, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain;charset=utf-8" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      redirect: "follow"
    });
    const text = await res.text();
    console.log(`✅ [Google 同步成功] 回應:`, text);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`⚠️ [Google 同步失敗] 原因:`, err.message);
  }
}

async function syncFromGoogleSheets() {
  try {
    console.log('🔄 正在從 Google 試算表同步資料...');
    const response = await fetch(`${GAS_URL}?t=${Date.now()}`, { redirect: "follow" });
    const data = await response.json();
    const db = loadDB();

    if (data.menu && data.menu.length > 0) db.menu = data.menu;
    if (data.settings) {
      db.settings = {
        ...db.settings,
        openTime: normalizeTime(data.settings.openTime || data.settings["營業時間起"]),
        closeTime: normalizeTime(data.settings.closeTime || data.settings["營業時間迄"]),
        closedDaysOfWeek: data.settings.closedDaysOfWeek || data.settings["每週公休"] || "3",
        tableNumberStart: parseInt(data.settings.tableNumberStart || data.settings["內用桌號起"]) || 1,
        tableNumberEnd: parseInt(data.settings.tableNumberEnd || data.settings["內用桌號迄"]) || 8,
        autoAccept: data.settings.autoAccept || data.settings["自動接單模式"] || db.settings.autoAccept || "關閉",
        enableDelivery: data.settings.enableDelivery || data.settings["外送服務"] || db.settings.enableDelivery || "開啟",
        promoDiscountCode: data.settings.promoDiscountCode || "vip8888",
        promoDiscountRate: parseFloat(data.settings.promoDiscountRate) || 0.9,
        promoDeductCode: data.settings.promoDeductCode || "",
        promoDeductAmount: parseInt(data.settings.promoDeductAmount) || 0,
        customerDisplayImages: data.settings["副螢幕輪播圖"] || data.settings.customerDisplayImages || ""
      };
    }
    if (data.announcement !== undefined) db.announcement = data.announcement;
    if (data.systemPassword) db.systemPassword = String(data.systemPassword);
    if (data.expenseQuickWords) db.expenseQuickWords = data.expenseQuickWords;
    if (data.incomeQuickWords) db.incomeQuickWords = data.incomeQuickWords;
    if (data.employees) {
      db.employees = typeof data.employees === 'string' ? data.employees.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : data.employees;
    }
    if (data.messages && Array.isArray(data.messages)) db.messages = data.messages;

    saveDB(db);
    broadcast({ type: 'MENU_UPDATE' });
    console.log('✅ 同步完成！目前自動接單模式：', db.settings.autoAccept);
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('⚠️ 從 Google 試算表同步失敗：', error.message);
    return false;
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------- [Google試算表同步核心邏輯] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [API路由：手動觸發試算表同步] 開始 ----------------------------------------
app.get('/api/pos/sync-sheets', async (req, res) => {
  const success = await syncFromGoogleSheets();
  res.json({ result: success ? 'success' : 'fail' });
});
// ---------------------------------------- [API路由：手動觸發試算表同步] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [API路由：GET讀取POS系統資料] 開始 ----------------------------------------
app.get('/api/pos', (req, res) => {
  const db = loadDB();
  const action = req.query.action;

  if (req.query.orderNumber) {
    const targetNum = cleanNum(req.query.orderNumber);
    const order = (db.orders || []).find(o => cleanNum(o.orderNumber) === targetNum || String(o.orderNumber) === String(req.query.orderNumber));
    if (order) {
      return res.json({
        result: 'success',
        status: order.status,
        paymentStatus: order.paymentStatus,
        pickupStatus: order.pickupStatus || '尚未取餐'
      });
    } else {
      return res.json({ result: 'not_found', status: '找不到訂單', paymentStatus: '尚未結帳' });
    }
  }

  if (action === 'getCustomerMsgs' && req.query.phone) {
    const phone = String(req.query.phone).replace(/\D/g, '');
    const myMsgs = (db.messages || []).filter(m => String(m.customerPhone || m.phone || '').includes(phone));
    return res.json({ result: 'success', messages: myMsgs });
  }

  if (action === 'getOrders') {
    return res.json({
      result: 'success',
      orders: db.orders || [],
      employees: db.employees || [],
      expenseQuickWords: db.expenseQuickWords || [],
      incomeQuickWords: db.incomeQuickWords || [],
      systemPassword: db.systemPassword || "1234",
      announcement: db.announcement || "",
      settings: db.settings || {},
      clockStatus: db.clockStatus || {},
      messages: db.messages || []
    });
  }

  res.json({
    menu: db.menu || [],
    settings: db.settings || {},
    systemPassword: db.systemPassword || "1234",
    announcement: db.announcement || "",
    expenseQuickWords: db.expenseQuickWords || [],
    incomeQuickWords: db.incomeQuickWords || [],
    employees: db.employees || [],
    clockStatus: db.clockStatus || {},
    messages: db.messages || []
  });
});
// ---------------------------------------- [API路由：GET讀取POS系統資料] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [API路由：POST處理POS核心業務] 開始 ----------------------------------------
app.post('/api/pos', (req, res) => {
  const db = loadDB();
  const body = req.body;
  const action = body.action;

  // 1. 新餐點訂單接收（嚴格排除收支，流水單號絕不跳號）
  if (!action && body.diningType) {
    const now = new Date();
    const targetDate = body.orderDate || now.toISOString().split('T')[0];
    const todayOrders = (db.orders || []).filter(o => 
      (o.orderDate === targetDate) && 
      !o.diningType.includes('支出') && 
      !o.diningType.includes('收入')
    );
    const nextNum = todayOrders.length + 1;
    const newOrderNumber = String(nextNum).padStart(3, '0');
    
    const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const hh = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const ii = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const timeStr = `${mm}/${dd} ${hh}:${ii}`;

    const newOrder = {
      id: Date.now(),
      orderNumber: newOrderNumber,
      orderDate: targetDate,
      time: timeStr,
      diningType: body.diningType,
      tableNumber: body.tableNumber || "",
      customerName: body.customerName || "現場客",
      customerPhone: body.customerPhone || "",
      detailsText: body.detailsText || "",
      totalAmount: body.totalAmount || 0,
      status: body.status || "⏳ 新訂單",
      paymentStatus: "尚未結帳",
      pickupStatus: "尚未取餐"
    };

    db.orders.unshift(newOrder);
    saveDB(db);
    broadcast({ type: 'NEW_ORDER', order: newOrder });
    sendToGoogle(body);

    const isAutoAccept = db.settings && (db.settings.autoAccept === '開啟' || db.settings['自動接單模式'] === '開啟');
    if (isAutoAccept && newOrder.status.includes('新訂單')) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const curDb = loadDB();
        const target = (curDb.orders || []).find(o => cleanNum(o.orderNumber) === newOrderNumber && o.orderDate === targetDate);
        if (target && target.status && target.status.includes('新訂單')) {
          target.status = '👩‍🍳 已接單';
          saveDB(curDb);
          broadcast({ type: 'STATUS_UPDATE', orderNumber: newOrderNumber, status: target.status });
          sendToGoogle({ action: "updateStatus", orderNumber: newOrderNumber, newStatus: target.status, orderDate: targetDate });
          console.log(`🔔 訂單 #${newOrderNumber} 已自動接單【👩‍🍳 已接單】`);
        }
      }, 5000);
    }

    return res.json({ result: 'success', orderNumber: newOrderNumber });
  }

  // 2. 訂單狀態更新
  if (action === 'updateStatus') {
    const targetNum = cleanNum(body.orderNumber);
    const target = db.orders.find(o => cleanNum(o.orderNumber) === targetNum || String(o.orderNumber) === String(body.orderNumber));
    if (target) {
      if (body.newStatus) target.status = body.newStatus;
      if (body.pickupStatus) target.pickupStatus = body.pickupStatus;
      if (body.paymentStatus) target.paymentStatus = body.paymentStatus;
      if (body.newStatus === "❌ 已取消") target.paymentStatus = "已取消訂單";

      saveDB(db);
      broadcast({ type: 'STATUS_UPDATE', orderNumber: target.orderNumber, status: target.status, pickupStatus: target.pickupStatus, paymentStatus: target.paymentStatus });
      sendToGoogle({ action: "updateStatus", orderNumber: target.orderNumber, newStatus: target.status, orderDate: body.orderDate || target.orderDate });
    }
    return res.json({ result: 'success' });
  }

  // 3. 結帳付款
  if (action === 'pay') {
    const targetNum = cleanNum(body.orderNumber);
    const target = db.orders.find(o => cleanNum(o.orderNumber) === targetNum || String(o.orderNumber) === String(body.orderNumber));
    if (target) {
      target.paymentStatus = "✅ 已結帳";
      saveDB(db);
      broadcast({ type: 'PAYMENT_UPDATE', orderNumber: target.orderNumber });
      sendToGoogle({ action: "pay", orderNumber: target.orderNumber, orderDate: body.orderDate || target.orderDate });
    }
    return res.json({ result: 'success' });
  }

  // 4. 系統設定更新
  if (action === 'updateSettings') {
    if (Array.isArray(body.settings)) {
      body.settings.forEach(item => {
        const k = item.key;
        const v = item.value;
        if (k === "店鋪公告") db.announcement = v;
        if (k === "營業時間起") db.settings.openTime = normalizeTime(v);
        if (k === "營業時間迄") db.settings.closeTime = normalizeTime(v);
        if (k === "每週公休") db.settings.closedDaysOfWeek = v;
        if (k === "特定公休日") db.settings.specialRestDates = v;
        if (k === "內用桌號起") db.settings.tableNumberStart = parseInt(v) || 1;
        if (k === "內用桌號迄") db.settings.tableNumberEnd = parseInt(v) || 8;
        if (k === "自動接單模式") db.settings.autoAccept = v;
        if (k === "打折優惠碼") db.settings.promoDiscountCode = v;
        if (k === "打折折數") db.settings.promoDiscountRate = parseFloat(v) || 0.9;
        if (k === "直扣優惠碼") db.settings.promoDeductCode = v;
        if (k === "直扣金額") db.settings.promoDeductAmount = parseInt(v) || 0;
        if (k === "外送服務") db.settings.enableDelivery = v;
        if (k === "支出常用詞清單") {
          db.expenseQuickWords = typeof v === 'string' ? v.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : v;
        }
        if (k === "收入常用詞清單") {
          db.incomeQuickWords = typeof v === 'string' ? v.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : v;
        }
        if (k === "員工名單") {
          db.employees = typeof v === 'string' ? v.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : v;
        }
        if (k === "進入系統密碼") db.systemPassword = String(v);
      });
    }
    saveDB(db);
    sendToGoogle({ action: "updateSettings", settings: body.settings });
    return res.json({ result: 'success' });
  }

  // 5. 菜單更新
  if (action === 'updateMenu') {
    db.menu = body.menu;
    saveDB(db);
    broadcast({ type: 'MENU_UPDATE' });
    sendToGoogle({ action: "updateMenu", menu: body.menu });
    return res.json({ result: 'success' });
  }

  // 6. 打卡紀錄
  if (action === 'clock') {
    if (!db.clockStatus) db.clockStatus = {};
    db.clockStatus[body.employeeName] = body.type;
    saveDB(db);
    sendToGoogle({ action: "clock", employeeName: body.employeeName, type: body.type });
    return res.json({ result: 'success' });
  }

  // 7. 收支記帳（🌟 關鍵修改：不生成餐點流水單號，不佔用任何單號空間）
  if (action === 'addTransaction') {
    const now = new Date();
    const mm = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const hh = String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const ii = String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const timeStr = `${mm}/${dd} ${hh}:${ii}`;

    const txOrder = {
      id: Date.now(),
      orderNumber: "", // 收支不設單號，避免餐點單號被污染
      orderDate: now.toISOString().split('T')[0],
      time: timeStr,
      diningType: body.type === 'expense' ? '💸 支出' : '💰 收入',
      customerName: body.note || '日常收支',
      detailsText: `${body.type === 'expense' ? '支出' : '收入'}項目：${body.note}\n金額：$${body.amount}`,
      totalAmount: body.amount,
      status: '✅ 已完成',
      paymentStatus: '✅ 已結帳'
    };
    db.orders.unshift(txOrder);
    saveDB(db);
    sendToGoogle({ action: "addTransaction", type: body.type, amount: body.amount, note: body.note });
    return res.json({ result: 'success' });
  }

  // 8. 客服訊息
  if (action === 'sendCustomerMessage' || action === 'sendMsg') {
    const now = new Date();
    const msgObj = {
      customerName: body.customerName,
      customerPhone: body.customerPhone,
      sender: body.sender || 'owner',
      text: body.content || body.text,
      time: `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`
    };
    if (!db.messages) db.messages = [];
    db.messages.push(msgObj);
    saveDB(db);
    sendToGoogle({
      action: "sendCustomerMessage",
      customerName: body.customerName,
      customerPhone: body.customerPhone,
      sender: msgObj.sender,
      content: msgObj.text
    });
    return res.json({ result: 'success' });
  }

  res.json({ result: 'success' });
});
// ---------------------------------------- [API路由：POST處理POS核心業務] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [健康檢查與靜態首頁路由] 開始 ----------------------------------------
app.get('/health', (req, res) => res.status(200).send('OK'));

app.get('/', (req, res) => {
  res.sendFile(path.join(publicPath, 'index.html'));
});
// ---------------------------------------- [健康檢查與靜態首頁路由] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [伺服器監聽啟動與初次同步] 開始 ----------------------------------------
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 8080;
server.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', async () => {
  console.log(`[POS 系統運行中] 服務端口: ${PORT}`);
  console.log(`開啟點餐網址: http://localhost:${PORT}/index.html`);
  console.log(`開啟收銀網址: http://localhost:${PORT}/pos.html`);
  await syncFromGoogleSheets();
});
// ---------------------------------------- [伺服器監聽啟動與初次同步] 結束 ----------------------------------------

  <!-- 左邊：圖片輪播區 -->
  <div class="left-promo" id="promo-container">
    <div class="promo-default" id="promo-default">
      <h1>日十。台中大里店</h1>
      <p>新鮮原型食物・保留古早味</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- 右邊：購物車對帳明細區 -->
  <div class="right-cart">
    <div class="cart-header">
      <div class="store-brand">日十。大里店</div>
      <div class="status-badge" id="screen-status">點餐中</div>
    </div>

    <div class="cart-items" id="items-box">
      <div class="cart-empty">
        <div class="icon">🍳</div>
        <div class="text">歡迎光臨！請確認點餐明細</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="cart-footer">
      <div class="footer-left" id="qty-total">共 0 項餐點</div>
      <div class="footer-right">
        <span class="total-title">合計：</span>
        <span class="total-price" id="total-val">$0</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // 1. 取得試算表中的圖片網址並每 6 秒自動輪播
    let promoImages = [];
    let currentIdx = 0;

    async function initPromo() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/pos?t=' + Date.now());
        const data = await res.json();
        const settings = data.settings || {};
        const urlsStr = settings['副螢幕輪播圖'] || settings.customerDisplayImages || '';

        if (urlsStr) {
          promoImages = urlsStr.split(',').map(u => u.trim()).filter(Boolean);
          startCarousel();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('輪播圖加載失敗:', err);
      }
    }

    function startCarousel() {
      const container = document.getElementById('promo-container');
      const defBox = document.getElementById('promo-default');
      if (promoImages.length === 0) return;

      if (defBox) defBox.style.display = 'none';
      container.innerHTML = '';

      promoImages.forEach((url, i) => {
        const img = document.createElement('img');
        img.src = url;
        img.className = 'promo-img' + (i === 0 ? ' active' : '');
        container.appendChild(img);
      });

      if (promoImages.length > 1) {
        setInterval(() => {
          const imgs = container.querySelectorAll('.promo-img');
          if (imgs.length === 0) return;
          imgs[currentIdx].classList.remove('active');
          currentIdx = (currentIdx + 1) % imgs.length;
          imgs[currentIdx].classList.add('active');
        }, 6000);
      }
    }

    initPromo();

    // 2. 監聽點餐購物車資料同步
    const cartChannel = new BroadcastChannel('pos_cart_channel');

    cartChannel.onmessage = (e) => {
      renderCart(e.data);
    };

    function renderCart(data) {
      const box = document.getElementById('items-box');
      const totalVal = document.getElementById('total-val');
      const qtyTotal = document.getElementById('qty-total');
      const statusBadge = document.getElementById('screen-status');

      if (!data || !data.items || data.items.length === 0) {
        box.innerHTML = `
          <div class="cart-empty">
            <div class="icon">🍳</div>
            <div class="text">歡迎光臨！請確認點餐明細</div>
          </div>`;
        totalVal.innerText = '$0';
        qtyTotal.innerText = '共 0 項餐點';
        statusBadge.innerText = data?.status || '點餐中';
        return;
      }

      statusBadge.innerText = data.status || '請核對餐點';
      let html = '';
      let sumQty = 0;

      data.items.forEach(it => {
        sumQty += it.qty;
        let optText = it.options ? `<div class="item-options">${it.options}</div>` : '';
        let noteText = it.note ? `<div class="item-note">備註：${it.note}</div>` : '';
        html += `
          <div class="item-card">
            <div class="item-info">
              <div class="item-name">${it.name}</div>
              ${optText}
              ${noteText}
            </div>
            <div class="item-qty">${it.qty}x</div>
            <div class="item-price">$${it.price * it.qty}</div>
          </div>
        `;
      });

      box.innerHTML = html;
      qtyTotal.innerText = `共 ${sumQty} 份餐點`;
      totalVal.innerText = `$${data.total || 0}`;
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>
<!-- ---------------------------------------- [副螢幕顧客對帳與輪播介面] 結束 ---------------------------------------- -->

    <div id="tx-split-container" style="display:none; width: 100%; height: 100%; padding: 20px; gap: 20px;">

          <div style="font-size: 1.5em; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 5px; color: #fca5a5;">💸 支出記帳</div>

// ---------------------------------------- [全域常數與狀態變數] 開始 ----------------------------------------
const GAS_URL = "https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbxTGrRGwx5aNc6LDKOkevrQVRN4Q_n8ERZqzUgi59cuALgED4pIjkcNMFDOIdQIrkcm/exec";
const API_URL = (window.location.hostname === "localhost" || window.location.hostname === "127.0.0.1") ? "/api/pos" : GAS_URL;

let shopSettings = { closedDaysOfWeek: "3", openTime: "06:00", closeTime: "13:00", promoDiscountCode: "vip8888", promoDiscountRate: 0.9, promoDeductCode: "", promoDeductAmount: 0 };
let menuData = []; 
let categories = [];
let cart = {}; 
let allOrders = []; 
let currentModalItem = null; 
let currentCategoryFilter = '全部';
let appliedPromo = null; 
let isChatOpen = false;
let todayMMDD = "";
let tomorrowMMDD = "";
// ---------------------------------------- [全域常數與狀態變數] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [日期與時間輔助函式] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function getMMDD(dateObj) {
    return String(dateObj.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '/' + String(dateObj.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
}

function getTodayDateString() {
    const d = new Date();
    return `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function getFormattedTime() {
    const now = new Date();
    return `${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')} ${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}
// ---------------------------------------- [日期與時間輔助函式] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [營業與公休狀態檢查] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function isDayClosed(dateObj) {
    const dayOfWeek = String(dateObj.getDay());
    const restDays = (shopSettings.closedDaysOfWeek || "").split(',').map(s => s.trim());
    return restDays.includes(dayOfWeek);
}

function isTodayOver() {
    const now = new Date();
    if (isDayClosed(now)) return true;
    const [closeH, closeM] = (shopSettings.closeTime || "13:00").split(":").map(Number);
    return (now.getHours() > closeH || (now.getHours() === closeH && now.getMinutes() >= closeM));
}
// ---------------------------------------- [營業與公休狀態檢查] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [載入店家設定與初始化] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function fetchSettingsAndInit() {
    fetch(`${API_URL}?t=${Date.now()}`)
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(data => {
        if (data.settings) shopSettings = { ...shopSettings, ...data.settings };
        if (data.announcement && data.announcement.trim() !== "") {
            document.getElementById("customer-announcement-banner").style.display = "block";
            document.getElementById("customer-announcement-text").innerText = data.announcement;
        }
        if (data.menu && data.menu.length > 0) {
            menuData = data.menu;
            categories = [...new Set(menuData.map(item => item.category))];
        }
        document.getElementById("loading-screen").style.display = "none";
        initApp(); 
    }).catch(err => {
        document.getElementById("loading-screen").innerHTML = `<h2>⚠️ 無法連線至店家</h2><p>請確認網路連線是否正常。</p>`;
    });
}
window.onload = fetchSettingsAndInit; 
// ---------------------------------------- [載入店家設定與初始化] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [顧客端應用程式初始化] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function initApp() {
    document.getElementById("menu-view").style.display = "block"; 
    document.getElementById("customer-name").value = localStorage.getItem('savedCustomerName') || "";
    document.getElementById("customer-phone").value = localStorage.getItem('savedCustomerPhone') || "";

    const todayObj = new Date();
    const tomorrowObj = new Date(); 
    tomorrowObj.setDate(tomorrowObj.getDate() + 1);
    todayMMDD = getMMDD(todayObj);
    tomorrowMMDD = getMMDD(tomorrowObj);

    const todayOver = isTodayOver();
    const tomorrowClosed = isDayClosed(tomorrowObj);
    const bannerContainer = document.getElementById("closed-banner-container");
    const submitBtn = document.getElementById("btn-submit-order");

    if (todayOver && tomorrowClosed) {
        bannerContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="closed-danger-banner">🛑 今日營業已結束，且明日為公休日<br><span style="font-size:0.85em; font-weight:normal;">目前全面暫停線上預訂服務，感謝您的支持！</span></div>`;
        if (submitBtn) {
            submitBtn.disabled = true;
            submitBtn.innerText = "目前暫停預訂服務";
        }
    } else if (todayOver) {
        bannerContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="closed-warning-banner">⚠️ 今日營業已結束，目前僅開放預訂【明日 (${tomorrowMMDD})】餐點</div>`;
    }

    renderPickupDateSelection(todayOver, tomorrowClosed);
    renderNavBar(); 
    renderMenu();
    updateCartUI(); 

    const savedOrders = localStorage.getItem('myBreakfastOrders');
    if (savedOrders) allOrders = JSON.parse(savedOrders);
    updateTicketFab();

    if (allOrders.length > 0) showStatusView(); else showMenuView();   
    
    loadMsgs(); 
    setInterval(() => loadMsgs(false), 5000); 
    setInterval(checkOrderStatus, 4000);
}
// ---------------------------------------- [顧客端應用程式初始化] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [更新浮動號碼牌按鈕與角標] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function updateTicketFab() {
    const ticketBtn = document.getElementById("fab-ticket");
    const badge = document.getElementById("ticket-badge");
    if (allOrders && allOrders.length > 0) {
        ticketBtn.style.display = "flex";
        badge.innerText = allOrders.length;
        badge.style.display = "flex";
    } else {
        ticketBtn.style.display = "none";
        badge.style.display = "none";
    }
}
// ---------------------------------------- [更新浮動號碼牌按鈕與角標] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [渲染取餐日期選項] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function renderPickupDateSelection(todayOver, tomorrowClosed) {
    const dateContainer = document.getElementById("date-selection");
    const labelAsap = document.getElementById("label-asap");
    let html = "";

    if (!todayOver) {
        html += `<label><input type="radio" name="pickupDate" value="${todayMMDD}" checked onchange="generateTimeOptions()"> 📅 今天 (${todayMMDD})</label>`;
        labelAsap.style.display = "flex";
    } else {
        labelAsap.style.display = "none";
    }

    if (!tomorrowClosed) {
        const checkedAttr = todayOver ? "checked" : "";
        html += `<label><input type="radio" name="pickupDate" value="${tomorrowMMDD}" ${checkedAttr} onchange="generateTimeOptions()"> 📅 明天 (${tomorrowMMDD})</label>`;
    }

    dateContainer.innerHTML = html;
}
// ---------------------------------------- [渲染取餐日期選項] 結束 ----------------------------------------

// ---------------------------------------- [重設取餐時間選項] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.resetTimeSelection = function() {
    const todayOver = isTodayOver();
    const rAsap = document.querySelector('input[name="timeType"][value="ASAP"]');
    const rSpec = document.querySelector('input[name="timeType"][value="SPECIFIC"]');

    if (rAsap) rAsap.checked = false;
    if (rSpec) rSpec.checked = false;

    document.getElementById("specific-time-container").style.display = "none";
    document.getElementById("pickup-time").value = "";

    if (todayOver) {
        if (rSpec) rSpec.checked = true;
        handleTimeTypeChange();
    }
};
// ---------------------------------------- [重設取餐時間選項] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [商品分類篩選與切換] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.filterCategory = function(categoryName) {
    currentCategoryFilter = categoryName;
    renderNavBar(); 
    
    if (categoryName === '購物車') {
        document.getElementById("cart-section").style.display = "block";
        document.querySelectorAll(".category-section").forEach(g => g.style.display = "none");
        resetTimeSelection();
    } else {
        document.getElementById("cart-section").style.display = "none";
        document.querySelectorAll(".category-section").forEach(g => {
            g.style.display = (categoryName === '全部' || g.id === `section-${categoryName}`) ? "block" : "none";
        });
    }
    document.getElementById("main-content").scrollTop = 0; 
};
// ---------------------------------------- [商品分類篩選與切換] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [更新購物車品項與數量] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.updateCart = function(cartKey, delta) {
    if (cart[cartKey]) {
        cart[cartKey].qty += delta;
        if (cart[cartKey].qty <= 0) delete cart[cartKey];
    }
    updateCartUI();
    if (typeof window.updateItemBadges === 'function') {
        window.updateItemBadges();
    }
};
// ---------------------------------------- [更新購物車品項與數量] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [更新購物車介面與金額計算] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function categorizeCustomerOrderSections(item) {
    const nameLines = (item.name || '').split(/\r?\n/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    const comboTitle = nameLines[0] || '';
    const subItems = nameLines.slice(1);

    let mainItemName = '';
    let drinkItemName = '';
    let rawSideItems = [];

    if (subItems.length > 0) {
        let foundDrink = false;
        subItems.forEach(line => {
            let isDrink = line.includes('飲') || line.includes('茶') || line.includes('咖啡') || line.includes('豆漿') || line.includes('拿鐵') || line.includes('冬瓜');
            if (isDrink && !foundDrink) {
                drinkItemName = line;
                foundDrink = true;
            } else if (!foundDrink) {
                if (!mainItemName) mainItemName = line;
                else rawSideItems.push(line);
            } else {
                rawSideItems.push(line);
            }
        });
    } else {
        mainItemName = comboTitle;
    }

    let mainModifiers = [];
    let drinkModifiers = [];
    let drinkPriceAdd = 0;
    let sideGroupsMap = {};
    let extraAddons = [];

    const opts = item.structuredOptions || [];
    opts.forEach(opt => {
        const title = opt.groupTitle || '';
        let extraText = opt.price > 0 ? `+${opt.price}` : '';
        let displayMod = `${opt.name}${extraText}`;

        if (title.includes('主餐')) {
            mainModifiers.push(displayMod);
        } else if (title.includes('飲料') || title.includes('飲品')) {
            drinkItemName = opt.name;
            drinkPriceAdd = opt.price || 0;
            if (opt.subModifiers && opt.subModifiers.length > 0) {
                drinkModifiers.push(...opt.subModifiers);
            }
        } else if (title.includes('加購') || title.includes('加點')) {
            extraAddons.push(displayMod);
        } else if (title.includes('副餐')) {
            let match = title.match(/副餐(\d+)/);
            let sideIdx = match ? parseInt(match[1], 10) : 1;
            if (!sideGroupsMap[sideIdx]) sideGroupsMap[sideIdx] = [];
            sideGroupsMap[sideIdx].push(displayMod);
        } else {
            if (title.includes('口感') || title.includes('加料') || title.includes('熟度')) {
                mainModifiers.push(displayMod);
            } else if (title.includes('冰') || title.includes('糖')) {
                drinkModifiers.push(displayMod);
            } else {
                if (!sideGroupsMap[1]) sideGroupsMap[1] = [];
                sideGroupsMap[1].push(displayMod);
            }
        }
    });

    let sideList = [];
    let maxSideCount = Math.max(rawSideItems.length, Object.keys(sideGroupsMap).length);
    for (let i = 1; i <= maxSideCount; i++) {
        let name = rawSideItems[i - 1] || (Object.keys(sideGroupsMap).length > 1 ? `副餐${i}` : '副餐');
        let mods = sideGroupsMap[i] || [];
        sideList.push({ name, mods });
    }

    return {
        isCombo: subItems.length > 0,
        comboTitle: subItems.length > 0 ? comboTitle : '',
        mainItemName,
        mainModifiers,
        drinkItemName,
        drinkPriceAdd,
        drinkModifiers,
        sideList,
        extraAddons,
        note: item.note || ''
    };
}

window.updateCartUI = function() {
    const cartDiv = document.getElementById("cart-items"); 
    const checkoutOptions = document.getElementById("checkout-options"); 
    const totalSpan = document.getElementById("total-price");
    
    let cartQty = 0;
    for(let key in cart) { cartQty += cart[key].qty; }
    const fabCart = document.getElementById("fab-cart");
    const cartBadge = document.getElementById("cart-badge");
    
    if (cartQty > 0) {
        fabCart.style.display = "flex";
        cartBadge.innerText = cartQty;
    } else {
        fabCart.style.display = "none";
    }
    
    if (Object.keys(cart).length === 0) { 
        cartDiv.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center; color:#94a3b8; font-weight:bold; padding:20px 0;">購物車是空的喔！</p>'; 
        totalSpan.innerText = "0"; 
        checkoutOptions.style.display = "none"; 
        document.getElementById("display-subtotal").style.display = "none";
        document.getElementById("discount-display").innerText = "";
        return; 
    }
    
    checkoutOptions.style.display = "block"; 
    cartDiv.innerHTML = ""; 
    let subtotal = 0;

    for (let cartKey in cart) {
        let item = cart[cartKey]; 
        let itemTotal = item.price * item.qty; 
        subtotal += itemTotal;
        
        const sec = categorizeCustomerOrderSections(item);
        let lines = [];

        if (sec.isCombo) {
            lines.push(`<div style="font-size:1.15em; font-weight:900; color:#0f172a; margin-bottom:4px;">${sec.comboTitle}</div>`);
            if (sec.mainItemName) {
                lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.95em; font-weight:800; color:#1e293b; padding-left:6px;">▸ ${sec.mainItemName}</div>`);
                if (sec.mainModifiers.length > 0) {
                    lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.88em; font-weight:700; color:#475569; padding-left:20px; margin-bottom:2px;">└ ${sec.mainModifiers.join(' / ')}</div>`);
                }
            }
        } else {
            lines.push(`<div style="font-size:1.1em; font-weight:900; color:#0f172a; margin-bottom:2px;">${sec.mainItemName}</div>`);
            if (sec.mainModifiers.length > 0) {
                lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.9em; font-weight:700; color:#475569; padding-left:12px; margin-bottom:2px;">└ ${sec.mainModifiers.join(' / ')}</div>`);
            }
        }

        if (sec.drinkItemName) {
            let drinkAdd = sec.drinkPriceAdd > 0 ? ` +${sec.drinkPriceAdd}` : '';
            lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.95em; font-weight:800; color:#1e293b; padding-left:${sec.isCombo ? '6px' : '0'};">▸ ${sec.drinkItemName}${drinkAdd}</div>`);
            if (sec.drinkModifiers.length > 0) {
                lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.88em; font-weight:700; color:#475569; padding-left:${sec.isCombo ? '20px' : '12px'}; margin-bottom:2px;">└ ${sec.drinkModifiers.join(' / ')}</div>`);
            }
        }

        sec.sideList.forEach(s => {
            lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.95em; font-weight:800; color:#1e293b; padding-left:${sec.isCombo ? '6px' : '0'};">▸ ${s.name}</div>`);
            if (s.mods.length > 0) {
                lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.88em; font-weight:700; color:#475569; padding-left:${sec.isCombo ? '20px' : '12px'}; margin-bottom:2px;">└ ${s.mods.join(' / ')}</div>`);
            }
        });

        if (sec.extraAddons.length > 0) {
            lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.9em; font-weight:800; color:#ea580c; margin-top:3px;">＋加購：${sec.extraAddons.join(' / ')}</div>`);
        }

        if (sec.note) {
            lines.push(`<div style="font-size:0.88em; color:#0284c7; font-weight:bold; margin-top:4px;">※ 備註：${sec.note}</div>`);
        }

        let blockHtml = lines.join('');

        cartDiv.innerHTML += `
            <div class="cart-item" style="padding:12px 4px; border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;">
                <div style="width:100%; line-height:1.55; word-break:break-word;">
                    ${blockHtml}
                </div>
                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-top:10px; padding-top:8px; border-top:1px dashed #f1f5f9;">
                    <div class="qty-control" style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;">
                        <button class="qty-btn" onclick="updateCart('${cartKey}', -1)">-</button>
                        <span class="qty-number" style="min-width:24px; text-align:center; font-weight:bold;">${item.qty}</span>
                        <button class="qty-btn" onclick="updateCart('${cartKey}', 1)">+</button>
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-weight: 900; font-size: 1.2em; color:#ea580c;">$${itemTotal}</div>
                </div>
            </div>`;
    }
    
    let finalTotal = subtotal;
    let discountAmt = 0;
    if (appliedPromo && subtotal > 0) {
        if (appliedPromo.type === 'discount') {
            discountAmt = Math.round(subtotal * (1 - appliedPromo.rate));
        } else {
            discountAmt = Math.min(subtotal, appliedPromo.amount);
        }
        finalTotal = Math.max(0, subtotal - discountAmt);
        
        document.getElementById("display-subtotal").style.display = "inline";
        document.getElementById("display-subtotal").innerText = "$" + subtotal;
        document.getElementById("discount-display").innerText = `🎟️ 優惠已折抵 -$${discountAmt}`;
    } else {
        document.getElementById("display-subtotal").style.display = "none";
        document.getElementById("discount-display").innerText = "";
    }
    
    totalSpan.innerText = finalTotal;
};
// ---------------------------------------- [更新購物車介面與金額計算] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [套用促銷優惠碼] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.applyPromoCode = function() {
    const inputEl = document.getElementById("promo-code-input");
    const input = inputEl.value.trim();
    const msgDiv = document.getElementById("promo-message");
    
    inputEl.value = "";

    if (!input) { 
        msgDiv.innerText = "⚠️ 請輸入優惠碼"; 
        msgDiv.style.color = "#ef4444"; 
        appliedPromo = null; 
        updateCartUI(); 
        return; 
    }

    if (shopSettings.promoDiscountCode && input.toLowerCase() === shopSettings.promoDiscountCode.toLowerCase()) {
        appliedPromo = { type: 'discount', rate: shopSettings.promoDiscountRate, code: input };
        msgDiv.innerText = `✅ 套用成功！享整單打折優惠`;
        msgDiv.style.color = "#10b981";
    } else if (shopSettings.promoDeductCode && input.toLowerCase() === shopSettings.promoDeductCode.toLowerCase()) {
        appliedPromo = { type: 'deduct', amount: shopSettings.promoDeductAmount, code: input };
        msgDiv.innerText = `✅ 套用成功！現折 $${shopSettings.promoDeductAmount} 元`;
        msgDiv.style.color = "#10b981";
    } else {
        appliedPromo = null;
        msgDiv.innerText = "❌ 優惠碼無效或已過期";
        msgDiv.style.color = "#ef4444";
    }
    updateCartUI();
};
// ---------------------------------------- [套用促銷優惠碼] 結束 ----------------------------------------

window.handleTimeTypeChange = function() {
    const isSpecific = document.querySelector('input[name="timeType"]:checked')?.value === "SPECIFIC";
    document.getElementById("specific-time-container").style.display = isSpecific ? "block" : "none";
    if (isSpecific) generateTimeOptions();
};

// ---------------------------------------- [生成可預約取餐時間列表] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.generateTimeOptions = function() {
    const timeSelect = document.getElementById("pickup-time");
    timeSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled selected>👉 請點此選擇時間</option>';
    const [openH, openM] = (shopSettings.openTime || "06:00").split(":").map(Number);
    const [closeH, closeM] = (shopSettings.closeTime || "13:00").split(":").map(Number);
    
    const selectedDate = document.querySelector('input[name="pickupDate"]:checked')?.value;
    const now = new Date();

    let minAllowedH = openH;
    let minAllowedM = openM;

    if (selectedDate === todayMMDD) {
        const curH = now.getHours();
        const curM = now.getMinutes() + 15;
        if (curH > openH || (curH === openH && curM > openM)) {
            minAllowedH = curH;
            minAllowedM = curM;
            if (minAllowedM >= 60) { minAllowedM -= 60; minAllowedH++; }
        }
    }

    let curH = openH;
    let curM = Math.ceil(openM / 15) * 15;
    if (curM >= 60) { curM = 0; curH++; }

    let hasValid = false;
    while (curH < closeH || (curH === closeH && curM <= closeM)) {
        if (curH > minAllowedH || (curH === minAllowedH && curM >= minAllowedM)) {
            let t = `${String(curH).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(curM).padStart(2,'0')}`;
            timeSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${t}">${t}</option>`;
            hasValid = true;
        }
        curM += 15;
        if (curM >= 60) { curM = 0; curH++; }
    }

    if (!hasValid) {
        timeSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled selected>⚠️ 此日期已無可預約時段</option>';
    }
    document.getElementById("shop-hours-text").innerText = `※ 營業時段：${shopSettings.openTime} ~ ${shopSettings.closeTime}`;
};
// ---------------------------------------- [生成可預約取餐時間列表] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [送出線上訂單至後端] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.submitOrder = function() {
    if (Object.keys(cart).length === 0) { alert("購物車是空的喔！"); return; }
    
    const timeTypeNode = document.querySelector('input[name="timeType"]:checked');
    if (!timeTypeNode) {
        alert("⚠️ 請先選擇【⏰ 取餐時間】要「盡快取餐」還是「指定預約時間」！");
        document.getElementById("datetime-container").scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        return;
    }

    let pickupTimeText = "";
    let orderDateBackend = getTodayDateString();

    if (timeTypeNode.value === "SPECIFIC") {
        const pickupDate = document.querySelector('input[name="pickupDate"]:checked')?.value;
        const specificTime = document.getElementById("pickup-time").value;
        if (!specificTime) { 
            alert("⚠️ 您選擇了指定預約時間，請務必選擇具體的取餐時間！"); 
            document.getElementById("pickup-time").focus();
            return; 
        }
        pickupTimeText = ` (預訂: ${pickupDate} ${specificTime})`;

        if (pickupDate === tomorrowMMDD) {
            const tmr = new Date(); tmr.setDate(tmr.getDate() + 1);
            orderDateBackend = `${tmr.getFullYear()}-${String(tmr.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(tmr.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
        }
    } else {
        pickupTimeText = " (盡快取餐)";
    }

    const customerName = document.getElementById("customer-name").value.trim();
    const customerPhone = document.getElementById("customer-phone").value.trim();
    if (!customerName) { alert("⚠️ 請填寫姓名！"); document.getElementById("customer-name").focus(); return; }
    if (!customerPhone) { alert("⚠️ 請填寫聯絡電話！"); document.getElementById("customer-phone").focus(); return; }
    
    localStorage.setItem('savedCustomerName', customerName);
    localStorage.setItem('savedCustomerPhone', customerPhone);

    const finalDiningType = `自取${pickupTimeText}`;
    let subtotal = 0;
    let details = "";
    let nl = "\n";

    for (let k in cart) {
        let item = cart[k];
        let itemTotal = item.price * item.qty;
        subtotal += itemTotal;

        const sec = categorizeCustomerOrderSections(item);
        let headerName = sec.isCombo ? sec.comboTitle : sec.mainItemName;
        details += `${item.qty}x ${headerName} ::: $${itemTotal}${nl}`;

        if (sec.isCombo) {
            if (sec.mainItemName) {
                details += `  ▸ ${sec.mainItemName}${nl}`;
                if (sec.mainModifiers.length > 0) details += `    └ ${sec.mainModifiers.join(' / ')}${nl}`;
            }
        } else {
            if (sec.mainModifiers.length > 0) details += `  └ ${sec.mainModifiers.join(' / ')}${nl}`;
        }

        if (sec.drinkItemName) {
            let drinkAdd = sec.drinkPriceAdd > 0 ? ` +${sec.drinkPriceAdd}` : '';
            details += `  ▸ ${sec.drinkItemName}${drinkAdd}${nl}`;
            if (sec.drinkModifiers.length > 0) details += `    └ ${sec.drinkModifiers.join(' / ')}${nl}`;
        }

        sec.sideList.forEach(s => {
            details += `  ▸ ${s.name}${nl}`;
            if (s.mods.length > 0) details += `    └ ${s.mods.join(' / ')}${nl}`;
        });

        if (sec.extraAddons.length > 0) {
            details += `  ＋加購：${sec.extraAddons.join(' / ')}${nl}`;
        }

        if (sec.note) details += `  ※ 備註：${sec.note}${nl}`;
    }
    details += `----------------------${nl}`;
    details += `小計 ::: $${subtotal}${nl}`;

    let finalTotal = subtotal;
    if (appliedPromo && subtotal > 0) {
        let discountAmt = appliedPromo.type === 'discount' ? Math.round(subtotal * (1 - appliedPromo.rate)) : Math.min(subtotal, appliedPromo.amount);
        finalTotal = Math.max(0, subtotal - discountAmt);
        details += `優惠折抵 ::: -$${discountAmt}${nl}`;
        details += `----------------------${nl}`;
    }
    details += `總額 ::: $${finalTotal}${nl}`;

    const needCutlery = document.getElementById("need-cutlery").checked ? "提供餐具" : "不提供餐具";
    const needStraw = document.getElementById("need-straw").checked ? "提供吸管" : "不提供吸管";
    details += `🍴 ${needCutlery} / 🥤 ${needStraw}${nl}`;

    const notes = document.getElementById("order-notes").value.trim();
    if (notes) details += `📝 全單備註：${notes}${nl}`;

    const backendData = {
        diningType: finalDiningType,
        tableNumber: "",
        customerName: customerName,
        customerPhone: customerPhone,
        detailsText: details,
        totalAmount: finalTotal,
        status: "⏳ 新訂單",
        orderDate: orderDateBackend
    };

    document.getElementById("loading-screen").innerHTML = `<h2>🔄 訂單傳送中...</h2><p>正在傳送給店家接單</p>`;
    document.getElementById("loading-screen").style.display = "flex";

    fetch(API_URL, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(backendData)
    })
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(data => {
        document.getElementById("loading-screen").style.display = "none";
        if (data.result === "success") {
            const newOrder = {
                id: Date.now(),
                orderNumber: data.orderNumber,
                orderDate: orderDateBackend,
                time: getFormattedTime(),
                diningType: finalDiningType,
                detailsText: details,
                totalAmount: finalTotal,
                status: 'pending'
            };
            allOrders.unshift(newOrder);
            localStorage.setItem('myBreakfastOrders', JSON.stringify(allOrders));

            cart = {};
            appliedPromo = null;
            document.getElementById("order-notes").value = "";
            updateCartUI();
            updateTicketFab();
            showStatusView();
        } else {
            alert("送單失敗，請稍後再試！");
        }
    })
    .catch(err => {
        document.getElementById("loading-screen").style.display = "none";
        alert("網路異常，請確認連線！");
    });
};
// ---------------------------------------- [送出線上訂單至後端] 結束 ----------------------------------------

// ---------------------------------------- [顧客端客服系統] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function parseChatDateAndTime(rawTime) {
    const now = new Date();
    const todayStr = `${now.getFullYear()}/${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const nowTimeStr = `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    if (!rawTime) return { date: todayStr, time: nowTimeStr };

    const str = String(rawTime).trim();
    const parts = str.split(/\s+/);
    if (parts.length >= 2) {
        let d = parts[0];
        if (/^\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}$/.test(d)) d = `${now.getFullYear()}/${d}`;
        let t = parts[1].slice(0, 5);
        return { date: d, time: t };
    }
    return { date: todayStr, time: str.slice(0, 5) };
}

// 1. 開啟客服對話視窗
window.openChat = function() {
    const modal = document.getElementById("chat-modal");
    if (!modal) return;

    modal.style.display = "flex";
    isChatOpen = true;

    // 清除右上角新訊息紅點提示
    // 清除右上角新訊息紅點與閃爍提示
  const badge = document.getElementById("fab-badge");
  const chatFab = document.querySelector(".fab-chat");
  if (badge) badge.style.display = "none";
  if (chatFab) chatFab.classList.remove("has-unread");

    const savedPhone = localStorage.getItem('chatCustomerPhone') || localStorage.getItem('savedCustomerPhone') || "";
    const savedName = localStorage.getItem('chatCustomerName') || localStorage.getItem('savedCustomerName') || "";

    if (savedPhone) {
        // 曾輸入過資訊，直接進入對話區
        document.getElementById("chat-login-area").style.display = "none";
        document.getElementById("chat-messages-area").style.display = "flex";
        document.getElementById("chat-input-area").style.display = "flex";
        loadMsgs(false);
    } else {
        // 首次使用，顯示姓名與手機填寫區
        document.getElementById("chat-login-area").style.display = "flex";
        document.getElementById("chat-messages-area").style.display = "none";
        document.getElementById("chat-input-area").style.display = "none";
        document.getElementById("chat-name").value = savedName;
        document.getElementById("chat-phone").value = savedPhone;
    }
};

// 2. 關閉客服對話視窗
window.closeChat = function() {
    const modal = document.getElementById("chat-modal");
    if (modal) modal.style.display = "none";
    isChatOpen = false;
};

// 3. 首次輸入姓名手機，進入對話
window.startChat = function() {
    const name = document.getElementById("chat-name").value.trim();
    const phone = document.getElementById("chat-phone").value.trim();

    if (!name) { alert("請輸入您的姓名"); return; }
    if (!phone) { alert("請輸入您的手機號碼"); return; }

    localStorage.setItem('chatCustomerName', name);
    localStorage.setItem('chatCustomerPhone', phone);
    localStorage.setItem('savedCustomerName', name);
    localStorage.setItem('savedCustomerPhone', phone);

    document.getElementById("chat-login-area").style.display = "none";
    document.getElementById("chat-messages-area").style.display = "flex";
    document.getElementById("chat-input-area").style.display = "flex";

    loadMsgs(false);
};

// 4. 切換身份 / 更換手機號碼
window.logoutChat = function() {
    localStorage.removeItem('chatCustomerPhone');
    document.getElementById("chat-login-area").style.display = "flex";
    document.getElementById("chat-messages-area").style.display = "none";
    document.getElementById("chat-input-area").style.display = "none";
    document.getElementById("chat-messages-area").innerHTML = "";
};

// 5. 輪詢與載入歷史對話訊息
let lastMsgCount = 0;
window.loadMsgs = function(showBadge = true) {
    const phone = localStorage.getItem('chatCustomerPhone') || localStorage.getItem('savedCustomerPhone');
    if (!phone) return;

    fetch(`${API_URL}?action=getCustomerMsgs&phone=${encodeURIComponent(phone)}&t=${Date.now()}`)
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.result === 'success' && Array.isArray(data.messages)) {
                const msgs = data.messages;
                const msgBox = document.getElementById("chat-messages-area");

		const ownerMsgsCount = msgs.filter(m => m.sender === 'owner').length;
                const savedReadOwnerCount = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('chatReadOwnerCount') || '-1', 10);
                const chatFab = document.querySelector(".fab-chat");
                const badge = document.getElementById("fab-badge");

                if (isChatOpen) {
                    localStorage.setItem('chatReadOwnerCount', String(ownerMsgsCount));
                    if (badge) badge.style.display = "none";
                    if (chatFab) chatFab.classList.remove("has-unread");
                } else {
                    const baseCount = savedReadOwnerCount === -1 ? (showBadge ? lastMsgCount : ownerMsgsCount) : savedReadOwnerCount;
                    const unread = Math.max(0, ownerMsgsCount - baseCount);
                    if (unread > 0) {
                        if (badge) {
                            badge.innerText = unread > 99 ? '99+' : unread;
                            badge.style.display = "flex";
                        }
                        if (chatFab) chatFab.classList.add("has-unread");
                    }
                }

                if (isChatOpen && msgBox) {
                    let html = "";
                    let lastDate = "";
                    msgs.forEach(m => {
                        const isOwner = (m.sender === 'owner');
                        const { date, time } = parseChatDateAndTime(m.time || m.timestamp || m.date);

                        if (date !== lastDate) {
                            lastDate = date;
                            html += `
                                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:center; margin:8px 0;">
                                    <span style="background:rgba(15,23,42,0.12); color:#475569; font-size:0.8em; font-weight:bold; padding:4px 14px; border-radius:16px;">${date}</span>
                                </div>
                            `;
                        }

                        html += `
                            <div class="chat-row ${isOwner ? 'owner-side' : 'customer-side'}">
                                ${isOwner 
                                    ? `<div class="chat-bubble owner">🏪 ${m.text || m.content}</div><div style="font-size:0.75em; color:#64748b; white-space:nowrap;">${time}</div>` 
                                    : `<div style="font-size:0.75em; color:#64748b; white-space:nowrap;">${time}</div><div class="chat-bubble customer">${m.text || m.content}</div>`
                                }
                            </div>
                        `;
                    });
                    msgBox.innerHTML = html;
                    msgBox.scrollTop = msgBox.scrollHeight;
                }
                lastMsgCount = msgs.length;
            }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
};

// 6. 顧客傳送訊息給店家
window.sendChatMsg = function() {
    const input = document.getElementById("chat-text");
    if (!input) return;
    const text = input.value.trim();
    if (!text) return;

    const phone = localStorage.getItem('chatCustomerPhone') || localStorage.getItem('savedCustomerPhone') || "";
    const name = localStorage.getItem('chatCustomerName') || localStorage.getItem('savedCustomerName') || "客人";
    const { date, time } = parseChatDateAndTime("");

    input.value = "";

    const msgBox = document.getElementById("chat-messages-area");
    if (msgBox) {
        if (!msgBox.innerHTML.includes(`>${date}</span>`)) {
            msgBox.innerHTML += `
                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:center; margin:8px 0;">
                    <span style="background:rgba(15,23,42,0.12); color:#475569; font-size:0.8em; font-weight:bold; padding:4px 14px; border-radius:16px;">${date}</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        msgBox.innerHTML += `
            <div class="chat-row customer-side">
                <div style="font-size:0.75em; color:#64748b; white-space:nowrap;">${time}</div>
                <div class="chat-bubble customer">${text}</div>
            </div>
        `;
        msgBox.scrollTop = msgBox.scrollHeight;
    }

    fetch(API_URL, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            action: "sendCustomerMessage",
            customerName: name,
            customerPhone: phone,
            sender: "customer",
            content: text
        })
    })
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(() => {
        loadMsgs(false);
    })
    .catch(() => {
        alert("訊息傳送失敗，請檢查網路連線");
    });
};
// ---------------------------------------- [顧客端客服系統] 結束 ----------------------------------------

// ---------------------------------------- [計算購物車商品總數] 開始 ----------------------------------------
function getItemCartCount(itemId) {
    if (typeof cart === 'undefined' || !cart || Object.keys(cart).length === 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    let total = 0;
    for (let key in cart) {
        const entry = cart[key];
        if (entry && typeof entry === 'object') {
            const matchesId = (entry.baseId !== undefined && entry.baseId !== null && String(entry.baseId) === String(itemId))
                           || (String(key).split('_')[0] === String(itemId));
            const validQty = parseInt(entry.qty, 10) || 0;
            if (matchesId && validQty > 0) {
                total += validQty;
            }
        }
    }
    return total;
}
// ---------------------------------------- [計算購物車商品總數] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [渲染側邊分類導航列] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.renderNavBar = function() { 
    const navDiv = document.getElementById("side-nav"); 
    if (!navDiv) return;
    let html = `<button class="nav-btn ${currentCategoryFilter === '全部' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="filterCategory('全部', true)">全部</button>`;
    categories.forEach(category => { 
        if (menuData.some(item => item.category === category)) {
            html += `<button class="nav-btn ${currentCategoryFilter === category ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="filterCategory('${category}', true)">${category}</button>`;
        }
    }); 
    navDiv.innerHTML = html;
};
// ---------------------------------------- [渲染側邊分類導航列] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [獨立更新品項角標] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.updateItemBadges = function() {
    if (typeof menuData === 'undefined' || !menuData) return;
    menuData.forEach(item => {
        const totalQty = getItemCartCount(item.id);
        const titleEl = document.getElementById(`item-title-${item.id}`);
        if (titleEl) {
            let badge = titleEl.querySelector('.item-badge');
            if (totalQty > 0) {
                if (!badge) {
                    badge = document.createElement('span');
                    badge.className = 'item-badge';
                    titleEl.appendChild(badge);
                }
                badge.textContent = totalQty;
            } else if (badge) {
                badge.remove();
            }
        }
    });
};
// ---------------------------------------- [獨立更新品項角標] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [渲染菜單清單] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.renderMenu = function() {
    const mainContent = document.getElementById("main-content");
    const currentScroll = mainContent ? mainContent.scrollTop : 0;
    const menuDiv = document.getElementById("menu"); 
    if (!menuDiv) return;

    let fullHtml = "";

    categories.forEach(category => {
        const items = menuData.filter(item => item.category === category);
        if (items.length > 0) {
            let html = `<div class="category-section" id="section-${category}"><div class="category-title">${category}</div><div class="category-items-grid">`;
            items.forEach(item => {
                const totalQty = getItemCartCount(item.id);
                const badgeHtml = totalQty > 0 ? `<span class="item-badge">${totalQty}</span>` : '';
                const isSoldOut = item.status === "售完" || (item.status && item.status.includes("完售")) || item.status === "停售";

                let rawName = item.name || '';
                let formattedTitle = rawName;
                if (!rawName.includes('\n')) {
                    if (rawName.includes('+')) formattedTitle = rawName.split('+').join('\n');
                    else if (rawName.includes(' ')) formattedTitle = rawName.split(' ').join('\n');
                }

                html += `
                    <div class="menu-item ${isSoldOut ? 'sold-out' : ''}" onclick="handleItemClick(${item.id})">
                        <div class="item-info">
                            <h3 id="item-title-${item.id}">${formattedTitle}${badgeHtml}</h3>
                        </div>
                        <span class="item-price">${isSoldOut ? '售完' : '$' + item.price}</span>
                    </div>`;
            });
            fullHtml += html + `</div></div>`;
        }
    });

    menuDiv.innerHTML = fullHtml;

    if (typeof filterCategory === 'function' && typeof currentCategoryFilter !== 'undefined') {
        filterCategory(currentCategoryFilter, false);
    }

    if (mainContent) {
        mainContent.scrollTop = currentScroll;
    }
};
// ---------------------------------------- [渲染菜單清單] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [品項點擊處理與開啟選項] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.handleItemClick = function(itemId) {
    const item = menuData.find(i => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) return;
    if (item.status === "售完" || (item.status && item.status.includes("完售")) || item.status === "停售") {
        alert("⚠️ 此商品今日已售完！");
        return;
    }
    // 不管有無客製化選項，皆彈窗讓顧客確認與填寫個人備註
    openModal(itemId);
};

window.handleModalOverlayClick = function(e) {
    if (e.target.id === 'option-modal') closeModal();
};

// 自動在單點菜單中比對飲料規格（甜度/冰塊）
function findDrinkModifiers(drinkName) {
    if (!drinkName || !menuData) return [];
    const cleanName = drinkName.replace(/\(.*?\)/g, '').trim();
    const matched = menuData.find(m => {
        const mName = (m.name || '').split('\n')[0].trim();
        return mName.includes(cleanName) || cleanName.includes(mName);
    });
    return (matched && matched.options) ? matched.options : [];
}

window.openModal = function(itemId) {
    currentModalItem = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(menuData.find(i => i.id === itemId))); 
    document.getElementById("modal-item-name").innerText = currentModalItem.name.split('\n')[0]; 
    document.getElementById("modal-base-price").innerText = `$${currentModalItem.price}`;
    
    renderModalOptionGroups();
    document.getElementById("option-modal").style.display = "flex"; 
    calcModalPrice(); 
};

function renderModalOptionGroups() {
    const item = currentModalItem;
    const container = document.getElementById("modal-options-container");
    if (!container || !item) return;
    
    let optionsHtml = "";

    (item.options || []).forEach((group, index) => {
        let rawTitle = (group.title || group.name || '').trim();
        let isRequired = group.required === true || rawTitle.startsWith("必選");
        let isMultiple = group.multiple === true || rawTitle.startsWith("複選") || rawTitle.startsWith("多選");
        let cleanTitle = rawTitle.replace(/^(必選|單選|複選|多選)[:：]?/, '').trim() || rawTitle;

        let badge = isRequired ? '<span style="color:#ef4444; font-size:0.8em; margin-left:6px; font-weight:bold;">*必選</span>' : (isMultiple ? '<span style="color:#10b981; font-size:0.8em; margin-left:6px; font-weight:bold;">(複選)</span>' : '<span style="color:#3b82f6; font-size:0.8em; margin-left:6px; font-weight:bold;">(單選)</span>');

        let isDrinkGroup = cleanTitle.includes("飲料") || cleanTitle.includes("飲品");

        optionsHtml += `<div class="option-group" data-group-index="${index}" data-required="${isRequired}" data-title="${cleanTitle}"><div class="option-group-title"><span>🍽️ ${cleanTitle}</span>${badge}</div>`;
        
        group.items.forEach((opt, optIdx) => {
            let p = parseInt(opt.price) || 0;
            let priceText = p > 0 ? `<span class="option-price">(+${p})</span>` : "";
            let inputType = isMultiple ? "checkbox" : "radio";
            let isChecked = (isRequired && optIdx === 0) ? "checked" : "";

            optionsHtml += `
                <div class="option-item" style="flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch;">
                    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                        <label style="flex:1; cursor:pointer;">
                            <input type="${inputType}" name="modal_group_${index}" value="${opt.name}" data-price="${p}" ${isChecked} onchange="handleModalOptionChange(${index}, '${opt.name}', ${isDrinkGroup})">
                            ${opt.name}
                        </label>
                        ${priceText}
                    </div>
                    ${isDrinkGroup ? `<div id="sub-modifiers-for-${index}-${optIdx}" class="sub-drink-box" style="margin-top:6px; padding:8px 12px; background:#f1f5f9; border-radius:8px; display:${isChecked ? 'block' : 'none'};"></div>` : ''}
                </div>`;
        });
        optionsHtml += `</div>`;
    });

    // 顧客單品專屬備註輸入框
    optionsHtml += `
        <div class="option-group" style="margin-top:16px;">
            <div class="option-group-title" style="border-left-color: #0284c7;">
                <span>📝 本項備註說明</span>
            </div>
            <input type="text" id="modal-item-note-input" class="table-input" style="margin-top:6px; font-size:1.05em;" placeholder="例如: 蛋餅焦一點、不要洋蔥、要蕃茄醬...">
        </div>
    `;

    container.innerHTML = optionsHtml;

    // 預設已選項目載入其規格
    (item.options || []).forEach((grp, idx) => {
        let cleanTitle = (grp.title || grp.name || '').replace(/^(必選|單選|複選|多選)[:：]?/, '');
        if ((cleanTitle.includes("飲料") || cleanTitle.includes("飲品")) && grp.items.length > 0) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                handleModalOptionChange(idx, grp.items[0].name, true);
            }, 0);
        }
    });
}

// 飲料選取時動態展開甜度與冰塊規格
window.handleModalOptionChange = function(groupIndex, selectedOptName, isDrinkGroup) {
    calcModalPrice();
    if (!isDrinkGroup) return;

    const grp = currentModalItem.options[groupIndex];
    grp.items.forEach((opt, oIdx) => {
        const box = document.getElementById(`sub-modifiers-for-${groupIndex}-${oIdx}`);
        if (!box) return;

        if (opt.name === selectedOptName) {
            box.style.display = 'block';
            const drinkOpts = findDrinkModifiers(selectedOptName);

            if (drinkOpts.length === 0) {
                let isChingTea = selectedOptName.includes("青茶");
                let isMilkTea = selectedOptName.includes("奶茶");

                let iceHtml = '';
                let sugarHtml = '';

                if (isChingTea) {
                    iceHtml = `<label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_ice" value="固定冰" checked onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 固定冰</label>`;
                    sugarHtml = `<label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_sugar" value="固定無糖" checked onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 固定無糖</label>`;
                } else if (isMilkTea) {
                    iceHtml = `
                        <label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_ice" value="冰" checked onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 冰</label>
                        <label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_ice" value="溫" onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 溫</label>
                    `;
                    sugarHtml = `<label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_sugar" value="甜度固定" checked onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 甜度固定</label>`;
                } else {
                    iceHtml = `
                        <label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_ice" value="冰" checked onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 冰</label>
                        <label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_ice" value="溫" onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 溫</label>
                    `;
                    sugarHtml = `
                        <label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_sugar" value="有糖" checked onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 有糖</label>
                        <label><input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_sugar" value="無糖" onchange="calcModalPrice()"> 無糖</label>
                    `;
                }

                box.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="font-size:0.85em; color:#64748b; font-weight:bold; margin-bottom:4px;">↳ 飲料客製：</div>
                    <div style="display:flex; gap:12px; flex-wrap:wrap; font-size:0.95em; font-weight:bold;">
                        ${iceHtml}
                        ${sugarHtml}
                    </div>
                `;
            } else {
                let subHtml = `<div style="font-size:0.85em; color:#64748b; font-weight:bold; margin-bottom:4px;">↳ ${selectedOptName} 規格：</div>`;
                drinkOpts.forEach((subGrp, sIdx) => {
                    const sTitle = (subGrp.title || subGrp.name || '').replace(/^(必選|單選|複選)[:：]?/, '');
                    subHtml += `<div style="margin-bottom:6px; font-size:0.9em; font-weight:bold; color:#334155;">${sTitle}：`;
                    subGrp.items.forEach((sItem, siIdx) => {
                        const chk = siIdx === 0 ? 'checked' : '';
                        subHtml += `
                            <label style="margin-right:8px; cursor:pointer;">
                                <input type="radio" name="sub_drink_${groupIndex}_${sIdx}" value="${sItem.name}" data-price="${sItem.price || 0}" ${chk} onchange="calcModalPrice()">
                                ${sItem.name}
                            </label>
                        `;
                    });
                    subHtml += `</div>`;
                });
                box.innerHTML = subHtml;
            }
        } else {
            box.style.display = 'none';
            box.innerHTML = '';
        }
    });
};

window.closeModal = function() { 
    document.getElementById("option-modal").style.display = "none"; 
    currentModalItem = null; 
};
// ---------------------------------------- [品項點擊處理與開啟選項] 結束 ----------------------------------------


// ---------------------------------------- [選項計價與確認加入購物車] 開始 ----------------------------------------
window.calcModalPrice = function() { 
    if (!currentModalItem) return;
    let total = currentModalItem.price; 
    document.querySelectorAll('#modal-options-container input:checked').forEach(inp => {
        total += parseInt(inp.getAttribute("data-price") || 0);
    });
    document.getElementById("modal-calc-price").innerText = total; 
};

window.confirmOptions = function() {
    const groups = document.querySelectorAll("#modal-options-container .option-group[data-group-index]");
    for (let g of groups) {
        if (g.getAttribute("data-required") === "true" && !g.querySelector("input:checked")) {
            alert(`⚠️ 請選擇【${g.getAttribute("data-title")}】！`);
            return;
        }
    }

    let structuredOptions = [];
    let addedPrice = 0;

    groups.forEach((grpEl, gIdx) => {
        const title = grpEl.getAttribute("data-title");
        const checkedList = grpEl.querySelectorAll(`input[name="modal_group_${gIdx}"]:checked`);
        checkedList.forEach(inp => {
            let p = parseInt(inp.getAttribute("data-price") || 0);
            addedPrice += p;

            let subModifiers = [];
            const parentItemDiv = inp.closest('.option-item');
            if (parentItemDiv) {
                const subBox = parentItemDiv.querySelector('.sub-drink-box');
                if (subBox && subBox.style.display !== 'none') {
                    subBox.querySelectorAll('input:checked').forEach(subInp => {
                        let sp = parseInt(subInp.getAttribute("data-price") || 0);
                        addedPrice += sp;
                        subModifiers.push(subInp.value);
                    });
                }
            }

            structuredOptions.push({
                groupTitle: title,
                name: inp.value,
                price: p,
                subModifiers: subModifiers
            });
        });
    });

    const noteInput = document.getElementById("modal-item-note-input");
    const itemNote = noteInput ? noteInput.value.trim() : "";

    const cartKey = `${currentModalItem.id}_${Date.now()}`; 
    const finalPrice = currentModalItem.price + addedPrice;

    if (!cart[cartKey]) {
        cart[cartKey] = {
            baseId: currentModalItem.id,
            name: currentModalItem.name,
            price: finalPrice,
            structuredOptions: structuredOptions,
            note: itemNote,
            qty: 1
        };
    } else {
        cart[cartKey].qty += 1;
    }

    closeModal();
    updateCartUI();
    if (typeof window.updateItemBadges === 'function') {
        window.updateItemBadges();
    }
};
// ---------------------------------------- [選項計價與確認加入購物車] 結束 ----------------------------------------
